# The test census

One line per section of every suite under tests/, read against tests/RUBRIC.md, in the rubric's record shape. The cull deletes by this file and never by the shape of a line. `## Culled` at the end lists every section the census culls.

rubric: 98b11b131

The header carries no totals: a count typed by hand is wrong the moment two landings add lines, so the counts are printed from the record lines. From the repository root, this prints the suites, the sections, one count per verdict, and the suites that have no section:

```
awk -F' [|] ' '$1 ~ /^[^ ]+\.test\.sh$/ && NF >= 5 { if (!($1 in s)) { s[$1] = 1; ns++ }
  if ($2 ~ /^§/) { n++; v[$3]++ } else if ($4 == "no section") nos++ }
  END { printf "suites: %d · sections: %d", ns, n; split("keep,duplicate,prose pin,tally,vacuous", w, ",")
  for (i = 1; i <= 5; i++) printf " · %s: %d", w[i], v[w[i]]; printf " · no section: %d suites\n", nos }' tests/CENSUS.md
```

The census leaves out the calls the section-list command counts inside heredoc bodies (framework.test.sh and runner-roster.test.sh write scratch suites, which are fixture text) and the fixture setup_sections, which build what a later section reads. A suite with no section has the one line `<suite> | — | — | no section | —`.

gate.test.sh | §SHARE | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh gate_share: a project's .bionic/config.yaml share: read ahead of the machine's share file, so project A reads 90 and the ask at 55 is admitted (S.3, S.5); also payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh _gate_share_cores: the `- r` reserve term dropped or gate_reserve's default made 0, so share 92 on 8 cores reads 7.36 and not 6.36 (S.19, S.20), or gate_state loses reserve= (S.21) | none (searched 'gate_share' and 'bionic/share' in tests/; the other suites plant a share and never a project line)
gate.test.sh | §WIDTH | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh gate_width: the floor of 1 dropped, so share 1 on 8 cores answers 0 (WD.4); also the core count taken as 8, so 64 cores answers 7 (WD.5); also gate_width reading the share without the reserve, so share 92, reserve 1, 8 cores answers 7 and not 6 (WD.7) | none (searched 'gate_width' in tests/; runner-width.test.sh §Section 6 reads 8 cores at shares 92 and 50 only)
gate.test.sh | §WIDTH-ASK | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh gate_width_ask: the oldest-landing rule dropped, so the younger LB is answered by the count while LA is active (WA.6, WA.9); also the count taken as max(busy, the promises) again, not the sum admission reads, so load 2.0 answers 4 and not 3 (WA.2), and the floor of 1 restored, so load 6.0 answers 1 (WA.3) | none (searched 'gate_width_ask' in tests/; landing-line-1.test.sh and landing-line-2.test.sh replace it with a stub) (WA.11 to WA.13, culled: with no floor WA.12 and WA.13 read what WA.3 reads and WA.11 what §LEASE LE.10 reads; WA.14 and WA.15 merged into one row)
gate.test.sh | §LEASE | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh gate_width_ask: the lease file unread, so LB is answered 4 in LA's gap between runs (LE.4, LE.27) | none (searched 'gate_landing_open' and 'leases/' in tests/)
gate.test.sh | §HARD | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh: the memory ceiling `fits(u, share)` made `fits(u, 100)`, so 85% used under a share of 80 is admitted (H.1, H.11); also _gate_decide's processor refusal given back its `unf > 0` guard, so 16 busy cores of 8 with nothing admitted is admitted (H.4) | none (searched 'fits(u, share)' and 'BIONIC_PROBE_USED_PCT' in tests/) (H.2, "its request was taken", culled: H.3 reads the same asked= beside its admitted line)
gate.test.sh | §ROOM | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh _gate_faded: the weight made 1 at every age, the promises summed whole as before, so three runs admitted 120 s ago at busy 2.95 read promised=4.83 room=no (RM.1) and 10 s ago 4.83 (RM.2); or `_gate_room_line` and `_gate_decide` reading different sums, so at the margin the room and an ask disagree (RM.3); or `num(busy) &&` dropped from _gate_faded's condition, so three runs admitted 30 s ago on an unreadable busy reading promise 2.4 and not the whole 4.83 (RM.5, with RM.4 the numeric control that still reads 2.4) | none (searched 'gate_room' and 'promised=' in tests/; stop.test.sh reads the tick's room line with nothing admitted, and the other hits are the landing record's promised= seconds)
gate.test.sh | §LONE | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh _gate_decide: the `unf > 0` guard restored, so the first ask at load 16 is admitted and the three do not all wait (LO.1); or `_gate_head`'s landing-before-work order inverted, so w1 is admitted before l1 (LO.2), or its asked= order reversed, so w2 goes before w1 (LO.3) | gate.test.sh §HARD (H.4 reads the guard at one ask)
gate.test.sh | §NOCONST | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh: the admission room's core count taken as 8 instead of the probe's, so the 6-core machine's third ask is refused (C.1) | none (searched 'BIONIC_PROBE_CORES' with gate_ask in tests/; §HARD and §TWO run on 8 cores)

gate.test.sh | §NUMBER | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh gate_ask: the resume of a handed-back request by the same who and key dropped, so the re-ask takes a fresh number and the later one is served first (N.9, N.11) | none (searched 'asked=' and 'resumed' in tests/gate.test.sh tests/booked.test.sh tests/slots.test.sh) (N.3 and N.7, culled: N.4 reads the same wait a tick later and N.6 the same asked=)

gate.test.sh | §CLASS | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh _gate_cores_for: the free-room prior replaced by gate_width, so a browser or whole ask at busy 2.0 is promised 6 and not 4 (CL.1); or --class unread, so the browser ask is promised the suite cost and the work ask beside it is admitted (CL.2) | none (searched '--class' and 'class=' in tests/: background-suite-guard.test.sh reads the wall's wrap text, not the gate's promise)
gate.test.sh | §REAPED | keep | payload/scripts/lib/gate.sh _gate_reap_fact: the call deleted, so a killed landing run is ended rc=137 and its run's log holds no run line (RP.1); or the record= test dropped, so a request naming no record writes one (RP.1) | none (searched 'rc=137' with 'run.log' in tests/)

## replay-bed.test.sh

replay-bed.test.sh | §TRACKED | keep | rule: lint — a fixture left untracked or deleted from tests/fixtures/replay, so the page, run log and gaps list, card file and gaps rows go red; a page left at 14 or an old home's file put back, so the version row goes red; the bed made to name the project's record store, so the store row goes red; an entry of the private list planted in a fixture, so tests/lib/name-scan.sh refuses it and the scan row goes red | none (searched 'fixtures/replay' and 'name-scan.sh' in tests/; release-check.test.sh plants entries in its own scratch repositories, not in the tracked fixtures)
replay-bed.test.sh | §CARDS | keep | payload/scripts/lib/reader.sh: any question's answer over a closed run moved off the card's fact (a landing, an approval, a proof, an ask, a finding, a review, a verdict count, a hand act, the order, the handoff, the ledger, a debt, the stop, a row's status or readiness), so that card's equality row goes red; the world's cut of the log made to keep a later line, so a card's landings, approvals or proofs differ | none (searched 'fixtures/replay' and '\.facts' in tests/; reader.test.sh reads planted logs and plans, never a closed run)
replay-bed.test.sh | §PAGES | keep | hooks/poker/plan.sh poker_verb_release_approval: a fact of the run read from the plan or the record instead of the reader (an approval, a waiver ask, a matrix waiver, a finding's state, the step a row is owed at, an open carry), so the card rendered at a card's instant differs from the replay table that card printed; the world's cut to the instant (its log, its rows born later) loosened, so the closed run's later facts reach the card; a waiver past the instant read, so the criterion pending by hand reads waived and its row goes red | none (searched 'release-approval-' and 'fixtures/replay' in tests/: release-approval.test.sh and release-approval-2.test.sh render planted plans, never a closed run at a card's instant)
replay-bed.test.sh | §CATCH | keep | payload/scripts/lib/reader.sh: the fold's replace rule (a later line with the same identity replaces the fact) or its cancel rule deleted, so the planted copy agrees with every card and the row goes red | reader.test.sh §FOLD (the same two rules on planted lines; this row is the closed runs' own catch, which AC-15.3 asks for)
replay-bed.test.sh | §COVER | keep | payload/scripts/lib/reader.sh: a question added with no assertion here, or one of the closed list refused as unknown; payload/scripts/lib/log.sh: an event type added that no fixture line holds; tests/fixtures/replay/hand-written.log: a line unmarked, or a type it alone holds gaining a real line | none (searched 'reader_list' and 'LOG_TYPES' in tests/; reader.test.sh refuses an unknown question but does not walk the list)
replay-bed.test.sh | §FOLLOWS | keep | hooks/poker/plan.sh poker_verb_brief: a review row's range started at another head than the reader's last review proof of its questions over a closed run, or its declared questions not rendered; payload/scripts/lib/fill.sh fill_facts_load not called by hooks/poker/tick.sh gate_report, so an approval or an order only the run's log holds leaves the tick's fill line as the plan's prose has it; hooks/poker/sitrep.sh sitrep_gather: the step, a decision or a review read from the plan or the design ledger instead of the reader, so a Step 9 the log retracts, a decision only the log puts or a review only the log holds is not on the sheet; hooks/poker/line.sh landing_report and poker_verb_release_note: the report or the note reading the landing record instead of the reader's lines, so a landing only a run's log holds is not counted or rendered, or the note's cut not applied, so a landing after its instant is rendered; hooks/poker/plan.sh poker_verb_release_approval: an approval read from the plan instead of the reader, so the release is still put once the log holds its approval, or still not put once the log cancels it; an approval a cancel appended to the log still read by the sheet's clock.approved or the tick's Step-3 approval | brief-file.test.sh §RENDER (the same range over a planted log; this row is the closed run's), none for the release card (searched 'ev=approval' and 'release-approval' in tests/: release-approval.test.sh reads approvals the plan holds, never one only the log holds)
replay-bed.test.sh | §OPEN | keep | payload/scripts/lib/run.sh run_open: the reader's step not asked, so a run whose log delivers it at step 9 or holds an abandon reads open, or a run whose log stands at step 8 reads closed | none (searched 'run_open' in tests/: run-predicate.test.sh pins the log's steps over planted pages)
replay-bed.test.sh | §FOLLOWS-CONTINUATION | keep | payload/scripts/close-out.sh: its debts read from the ledger file instead of the reader's facts, so a debt the run's log alone holds is missing from the card's debt line and the continuation's Deferrals; its step read from the plan's prose instead of the reader, so the reopened run is judged on a step its log does not hold, and a run its log takes back to Step 7 reads as at Step 8 | none (searched 'close-out.sh' and 'run.log' in tests/: the close-out suites plant version-14 plans with no log)
replay-bed.test.sh | §AGREE | keep | hooks/poker/sitrep.sh sitrep_gather or hooks/poker/plan.sh's Reviews lines: the reviews counted a second way, so in a card's world the sheet's reviews line, passed and failed, differs from the card's Reviews lines; or sitrep_so_far (the line both print) not asked by one of them, so its acts taken by hand or open asks differ from the other's or from the reader's hands and asks at the log the card read | release-approval-2.test.sh COVERED-0c (the failed count alone, over one planted plan; this row is the closed runs', passed and failed); none for hands and asks (searched 'so-far' and 'reader_q_hands' in tests/)
replay-bed.test.sh | §HALF | keep | payload/scripts/lib/reader.sh reader_events: a run not brought forward read from its log alone, so the cut run's landings, step and approval are lost; lib/translate.sh _translate_log unregistered, so the logged landing or the cancel is not read; lib/run.sh run_open asking the log of a run not brought forward | reader.test.sh §HALF (the same rule on planted homes; this row drives the sheet, the tick and run_open over a real cut run)
